# Command: ip multicast multipath

# Command: ip pim sg-expiry-timer infinity


| sg-expiry-timer | Adjust expiry time for PIM ASM (S,G) routes |
| expiry-time | Expiry timer interval in seconds |
| infinity | Never expire (S,G) route due to data inactivity |
| sg-list | (Optional) Specifies route-map for (S,G)s to apply the expiry timer |

| prefix-list | (Optional) Specifies prefix-list for (S,G)s to apply the expiry timer |
